# Business Requirements: Tegg, a Local-First Object Note-Taking App

|                     |                                                                     |
| ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Document status** | Draft v1.152, for review                                            |
| **Date**            | 2026-11-01                                                          |
| **Source**          | `initial requirements.md` + clarification session (decisions in §3) |
| **Working name**    | **Tegg**                                                            |

> **How to read this document.** Every requirement has an ID (e.g. `FR-TSK-04`) and a priority:
> **M** = Must (v1), **S** = Should (v1 if possible), **C** = Could (later), **W** = Won't (explicitly out of scope for now).
> Items marked ❓ depend on an open question in §13. Each open question carries a recommendation that I will assume if nobody answers it.

| v1.152  | 2026-11-07 | Slice PIN red-test decisions D-1393…D-1404 (AP-1250…AP-1261, as proposed): duplicates, the last unpin, Rust stubs, the perf test nightly, the merge job patches pins, row shape |

---

## 1. Vision & Goals

### 1.1 Vision

A fast, clean, modern **object-based** note-taking app for people who work with hundreds to thousands of notes of different kinds: reading notes, Zettelkasten, working and meeting notes, planning (e.g. trips). It has the structured "everything is an object" experience of Anytype and Capacities, while every note stays a **plain Markdown file on the user's disk**. No vendor lock-in and no proprietary cloud.

### 1.2 Business goals

| ID  | Goal                              | Measure of success                                                                                                                      |
| --- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| G-1 | Vendor independence               | 100% of user content is readable without the app (Markdown + YAML + image/PDF files), and the vault opens in Obsidian with no data loss |
| G-2 | Speed                             | Any note reachable in < 1 s via keyboard; UI never blocks while typing (see NFR-PERF)                                                   |
| G-3 | Structure without friction        | Converting a paragraph into a typed object (e.g. a Task) takes ≤ 2 actions                                                              |
| G-4 | Trustworthy capture → review loop | Every captured item has a visible review state; the inbox can be emptied                                                                |
| G-5 | Growth path                       | The architecture allows mobile clients (platform open, D-100), API sync and AI features later without a rewrite                                           |

### 1.3 Non-goals (for v1)

- No proprietary cloud, server or account system.
- No real-time multi-user collaboration.
- No AI features in v1 (the architecture must not block them; see §11).
- No mobile apps in v1 (the architecture must allow them).

---

## 2. Users & Personas

| Persona                     | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Priority                                      |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| **P1: The owner (primary)** | Knowledge worker on an Apple Silicon Mac. Keeps reading notes, a Zettelkasten, meeting/working notes and plans. Wants keyboard speed, structure and data ownership. Uses Dropbox/Google Drive desktop clients. | v1 target                                     |
| **P2: Future public user**  | Similar profile, possibly migrating from Obsidian, Anytype, Capacities or Notion. Needs onboarding, import, and a polished, documented product.                                                                | Design must not block it; not built for in v1 |

**Implication of "me first, maybe others later":** v1 can skip licensing, payments, accounts, auto-update infrastructure and support tooling. It still must meet code-signing/notarization, data-safety and import requirements so that a later public release is possible without a rewrite.

---

## 3. Decision Log (confirmed with the owner)

| #    | Topic                                | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---- | ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| D-01 | Audience                             | Me first, possibly a public product later                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-02 | Mobile                               | Mobile planned (future phase); **platform open** (superseded by D-100, was: iOS and Android)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| D-03 | Sync                                 | Phase 1: rely on the Dropbox/Google Drive **desktop clients** syncing the vault folder. Phase 2: built-in API sync (needed for mobile)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-04 | Editor                               | Block-based object editor in the style of **Anytype / Capacities**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| D-05 | File format                          | **Obsidian-compatible**: YAML frontmatter for attributes, `[[wikilinks]]`, `![[embeds]]`, plain attachment files. Mindmaps stored as **Mermaid** code blocks (superseded by D-679: a `> [!mindmap]` callout holding a list)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| D-06 | Scale                                | Up to ~5,000 notes (plus attachments) within 3 years                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| D-07 | Tasks                                | Status, due date, priority, recurrence, board view and calendar view. Tasks must keep a link to their **source note** and be manageable from Task views                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| D-08 | Block conversion                     | A converted block becomes its **own .md file**; the source note keeps an **embed** in its place; the new object records its source                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| D-09 | Queries                              | **Visual query builder** (no text query language required)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| D-10 | Import                               | Obsidian vault; Anytype / Capacities / Notion exports                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| D-11 | Export                               | PDF, HTML, DOCX, Markdown bundle (zip)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-12 | AI                                   | Later phase; don't block it architecturally                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| D-13 | Folders                              | **App manages folders by object type**; users navigate by type/query, not folder tree                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| D-14 | Quick Capture entry points           | **Menu bar icon**, **macOS Share sheet**, and **global hotkeys** for Quick Capture and New Task (OQ-24). Web clipper out of scope                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| D-15 | Quick notes vs daily notes           | **Both exist with distinct roles.** Quick Note = inbox item; Daily Note = dated hub. **Both have a review status** and can be marked reviewed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| D-16 | Templates                            | Attribute defaults, body structure, variables, note-scoped queries, multiple templates per type, applied at creation only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-17 | v1 extras                            | Daily notes, templates per type, version history + trash                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| D-18 | Attachments                          | **Images + PDF** in v1                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-19 | Tech stack                           | **Tauri 2 + React/TypeScript + ProseMirror/TipTap + SQLite**, confirmed (OQ-16; see §8.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-20 | Platform v1                          | macOS on Apple Silicon (ARM64)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| D-21 | Working name                         | **Tegg** (OQ-01)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| D-22 | System types                         | **Page**, **Task**, **Quick Note**, **Daily Note**, **Tag**, **Image**, **PDF** (Image and PDF from D-42; list unified by D-89). Users may **add** attributes to system types but cannot change or remove built-in ones (OQ-02, OQ-04, OQ-17)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-23 | New vault setup                      | User chooses an **empty vault** (system types only) or a **starter vault** with Book, Reading Note (D-92), Zettel (Idea), Meeting, Person, Project, Trip, all editable (OQ-03)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| D-24 | Object typing                        | Exactly one type per object, changeable. **Default type for new notes is set per vault; default = Quick Note** (OQ-25)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-25 | Task status & priority               | Configurable labels and colors; each status maps to a fixed semantic state: open / in progress / done / cancelled (OQ-05)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-26 | Recurring tasks                      | Completing a recurring task creates the next occurrence as a **new file**; the done one stays as history (OQ-06)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| D-27 | Embed / object deletion              | Removing an embed doesn't delete the object (ask once, "don't ask again"). A trashed object shows a "moved to trash" placeholder with Restore (OQ-07)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| D-28 | Checklists vs tasks                  | Only Task objects appear in Task views; checklist items can be converted in one action (OQ-08)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| D-29 | Tags                                 | Tag is a **system object**; the user can change its name and color (OQ-17)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| D-30 | Review status                        | **Mandatory** on Quick Notes and Daily Notes; **opt-in per type** for all other types (OQ-18)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| D-31 | Version history                      | Stored **locally** (not synced), kept **90 days**; the sync provider's file versioning is the cross-device backup (OQ-19)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-32 | Platform & distribution              | English UI, prepared for localization (OQ-20); direct-download notarized DMG, **macOS 14+** (OQ-21); no encryption, rely on FileVault (OQ-22)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| D-33 | Reading notes                        | No built-in reading-specific features; the user defines reading types as needed (OQ-23)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| D-34 | Out of v1                            | Web clipper (OQ-24); task reminders/notifications (OQ-26)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-35 | Later (v1.x)                         | Multiple vaults (OQ-09); customizable Home (OQ-10); split view (OQ-11); global graph (OQ-14). In v1: single vault, fixed Home, **tabs**, local graph                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| D-36 | Files & locations                    | Obsidian vault: open in place **or** copy (OQ-12); file name = sanitized title (OQ-13); mindmap visual editor saving to Mermaid (OQ-15; the format is a list since D-679); iCloud Drive supported as a folder location now, API in Phase 2 (OQ-27)                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| D-37 | Object previews                      | **Click previews, double-click opens**, everywhere (editor, embeds, lists, graph). The trigger is a global app setting (OQ-28)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| D-38 | Preview content                      | Previews are **content-first**: they must show what the object is about (its content), with attributes secondary (OQ-30)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| D-39 | Object icon & cover                  | Each object can have its **own icon/emoji and cover image**, falling back to the type icon (OQ-29)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| D-40 | Tags & hotkeys                       | **No nested tags** (OQ-31); separate global **New Task** hotkey confirmed (OQ-32)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| D-41 | Capacities import in MVP (superseded by D-801: v1.0) | The **Capacities importer is part of the MVP** (v0.1), so the owner can move their existing Capacities data into Tegg from day one. Notion and Anytype import stay in v1.x, Obsidian in v1.0 (see §11.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| D-42 | Images & PDFs                        | **Every image and PDF is an object** (system types Image and PDF) with title, description, tags and links (OQ-33)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| D-43 | Weblinks                             | **No Weblink object type** and no fetching of link metadata. Imported Capacities weblinks become ordinary objects with a `url` attribute (OQ-34)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| D-44 | Capacities spaces                    | The user **selects one Capacities space per import**; other spaces can be imported in separate runs (OQ-35)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| D-45 | Page hierarchy                       | **No parent/child page support** (OQ-36)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| D-46 | Task dates                           | Tasks have **only a due date** (OQ-37)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-47 | Notion people                        | Imported as **plain text** (OQ-39)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| D-48 | Collections                          | No tag groups/collections in the MVP. Possibly later as a new **Collection** system object (OQ-40)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| D-49 | Re-import                            | MVP: **one-time import**; importing a newer export again is treated as a new import. Add-new-only or update re-import may come in v1.x, using the stored `import-id` (OQ-38)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| D-50 | Test data                            | **No anonymizer.** Tests use hand-written synthetic fixtures (committed) plus a local-only run against the real exports (never committed) (FR-IMP-23)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| D-51 | File-name length                     | File names (without `.md`) are limited to **200 UTF-8 bytes**, cut at a character boundary; the full title stays in `title:` (AP-1, NFR-POR-02)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| D-52 | Forbidden characters                 | `/ : \ * ? " < > \|` in titles are replaced by a space (and `# ^ [ ]`, D-68); repeated spaces collapsed; leading/trailing spaces and dots trimmed (AP-2, FR-FMT-08)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| D-53 | Conflict copies                      | A file is a conflict copy only if it has the **same `id`** as another file; provider name patterns are only hints. Tegg duplicate suffixes start at `(2)` (AP-3, FR-SYN-04/05, FR-LNK-14)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-54 | First `id`                           | `id` and `type: page` are written to an untyped file on its **first edit** in Tegg; until then the index identifies it by path (AP-4, FR-FMT-02, FR-TYP-10)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| D-55 | `modified` timestamp                 | Updated only when content or an attribute actually changed (AP-5, FR-SYN-06)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| D-56 | Imported date-times                  | UTC date-times are converted to the **local time zone at import**, written with offset (AP-6, FR-FMT-11, FR-IMP-09)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| D-57 | MVP restore points                   | Until version history (v1.0), a **vault backup zip** in Application Support is created before every bulk operation (import, attribute rename, tag rename, bulk type change); newest 10 kept (AP-7, FR-HIS-04, NFR-REL-04)                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-58 | Performance reference                | Apple M1, 8 GB RAM, macOS 14, release build; generated 5,000-note reference vault; 95th percentile of ≥ 20 runs (AP-8, `docs/acceptance/performance.md`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| D-59 | Performance reference (amends D-58)  | Reference machine is the owner's **Apple M2 Pro, 16 GB, macOS 26**, release build, p95 of ≥ 20 runs. Because it is faster than an M1 8 GB, latency targets are tightened (initially 75% of the NFR value, full index 60%, memory unchanged) and a **slow profile** (efficiency cores only) is reported against the original values. Factors are calibrated in spike S4 (`docs/test-strategy.md` §9, `docs/acceptance/performance.md`) |
| D-60 | Test execution                       | **Local only**: one `./verify` script with gates G0 commit, G1 push, G2 nightly, G3 milestone, G4 release, enforced by git hooks and Claude Code hooks. No hosted CI until a public release or a second contributor (`docs/test-strategy.md` §8)                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| D-61 | Claude in QA                         | Claude acts as AC author, test author, QA reviewer and exploratory tester, separate from the implementing session. AC tests are written from ACs and interfaces only, committed **red** before the implementation, and may be changed only with owner approval (`AC-Test-Change:` trailer). Mutation-score thresholds T1 / T2: 70% / 60% in MS1–MS3, 80% / 70% from MS4 (`docs/test-strategy.md` §6, §11)                             |
| D-62 | QA operations                        | Nightly gate via `launchd` under `caffeinate`; Claude is **denied access to `test exports/`** and the owner starts the real-export run; data-loss (T1) bugs stop feature work during the MVP; iCloud Drive is enabled on both Macs before MS8 to test iCloud sync                                                                                                                                                                     |
| D-63 | MVP milestones                       | The MVP is built in milestones **MS1–MS8**, each with its own automated test gate (§11.2)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| D-64 | Tags in math                         | `#tags` inside inline math (`$…$`) and display math (`$$…$$`) are **not tags**, like code and links (AP-15, spike S4; dialect §5)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| D-65 | Slow profile                         | The slow profile (`taskpolicy -b`, efficiency cores) is a **reported worst case with no pass/fail target**; the 75% / 60% factors of D-59 stay (AP-16, spike S4). A one-off measurement on an M1 can confirm them if one is available                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| D-66 | Typing latency metric                | NFR-PERF-06 is measured in frames: **≥ 95% of keystrokes painted in the next frame, none later than two frames**; the D-59 factor is not applied to it, because below one frame it would measure the display, not the app (AP-9, spike S2)                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| D-67 | Save may touch an unedited neighbour | Where deleting or moving a block would merge two lists, **one list's marker changes** (`-` → `*`, `1.` → `1)`); where a list is newly followed by indented code, a `<!-- -->` line is inserted (also between two lists that both hold an embed line, D-1020). Both are the minimal change that keeps the note's structure and are the only exceptions to FR-FMT-07 (AP-10, ADR 0002, dialect §1)                                                                                                    |
| D-68 | Obsidian-safe file names             | D-52 also replaces `# ^ [ ]` with a space in file names, because Obsidian can't link to names containing them; the full title stays in `title:` (AP-11)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| D-69 | Capture save key                     | In the capture window **⌘↩ saves and closes, Enter inserts a new line** (FR-CAP-01; AP-12)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| D-70 | Task and capture keys                | `captured` is a reserved key (FR-TYP-15). The Task type's attribute keys are `status`, `priority`, `due`, `completed` and `recurrence`, defined in the dialect (§3) before MS5 (AP-13)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-71 | Single instance, hotkey conflicts    | The app runs as a **single instance** (a second launch focuses the first). A global hotkey that can't be registered is shown in Settings with a prompt to choose another (FR-CAP-05, FR-SET-04; AP-14)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| D-72 | Where properties are defined         | An object's properties come only from its type. Properties are **added, renamed or removed only on the type's Type page** (the "all objects" view, e.g. its add-column button) **or in the type settings**, never from a single object: the attribute panel on an object edits values only (FR-EDT-07, FR-TYP-09). Frontmatter keys outside the type's schema (Obsidian files, type changes, deleted attributes) are still kept and shown under "Other properties" (FR-TYP-04, FR-TYP-07) |
| D-73 | Link style                           | A link is one inline chip, **the same everywhere** (editor text, attribute fields, tables, backlinks): object or type icon + title in the link colour, **regular weight**, on a **light link-coloured background tint**, no border. The tint is not a frame, so links stay distinct from framed embeds (FR-LNK-07). Broken links have no tint: error colour with a dashed underline (FR-LNK-04) |
| D-74 | Task embed shows its content         | The default Task embed is a **card**: a header row (checkbox, title, status, priority, due date, open button) with the **task's body below it**, editable in place (FR-EMB-02, FR-EMB-03, FR-CNV-05). A plain checklist item (`- [ ]`) stays a Markdown checkbox until the user converts it (FR-TSK-11, FR-CNV-01) |
| D-75 | Media `source` on import             | Capacities `source: upload` (plain text) on Image/PDF objects is renamed to `source-text` like every plain-text `source` (FR-TYP-15); FR-IMP-14 AC-1 updated (AP-22) |
| D-76 | Duplicate-title wikilinks on import  | A Capacities `[[Title]]` matching several objects points at the **first by source path**, is marked, and is listed in the import report as "ambiguous" (AP-23, FR-IMP-10, FR-LNK-14) |
| D-77 | Unreferenced media on import         | A media file no note references is imported into `_attachments/` **and** gets an Image/PDF object (title = file name), listed in the report (AP-24, D-42, FR-IMP-14) |
| D-78 | Technical attributes on import       | Image/PDF `mimeType`, `fileSize`, `width`, `height`, `colors` and Weblink `domain`, `iframeUrl` are **dropped by default** (reported, changeable in the mapping) (AP-25, FR-IMP-08, FR-IMP-24) |
| D-79 | Export file classes                  | `.md` = object (FR-IMP-03); `.csv` = tag collection or other file, reported (FR-IMP-21); dotfiles (incl. hidden `.md`) = ignored and reported; every other file = attachment (FR-IMP-14). Basis of the §11.1 accounting rule (AP-26) |
| D-80 | Reserved and empty file names        | A file stem reserved on Windows (`CON`, `PRN`, `AUX`, `NUL`, `COM1`…`COM9`, `LPT1`…`LPT9`, case-insensitive) gets `_` appended (`CON_.md`); a title with no valid characters is named `Untitled` (then ` (2)` …); the title stays in `title:` (AP-27, FR-FMT-08, NFR-POR-02, D-52) |
| D-81 | Case-insensitive link matches        | A link whose only match is a title differing in case is **not resolved silently**: the import preview shows it for the user to confirm (AP-28, FR-IMP-10, FR-LNK-14) |
| D-82 | One text engine                      | Every new note's bytes (typing, attribute edits, bulk renames, import body conversion) are produced by the TypeScript document engine `@tegg/doc`; Rust never edits Markdown text. Rust orchestrates bulk jobs (file selection, backup, conflict checks, atomic writes) and runs transformations through a Web Worker in the main webview; the import planner stays in Rust (AP-29, ADR 0003, `docs/architecture.md`) |
| D-83 | Spike code in the product            | Spike modules are **ported** into product packages deliberately, with tests re-authored from the ACs by the test author (D-61); product code never imports from `spikes/` (L0 check) (AP-31) |
| D-84 | Residual overwrite window            | The sub-millisecond window between the disk-version check and the atomic rename, in which an external write could be replaced, is **accepted as a documented limit** of NFR-REL-02 (no lock is respected by sync clients); from v1.0, version history keeps the replaced bytes so even that case is recoverable (AP-30, `docs/architecture.md` §5.3) |
| D-85 | MVP requirements without a milestone | The 27 Must/NFR/AR requirements the MVP needs but no milestone owned are assigned to milestones in §11.2: default type, embeds, core settings, UI rules incl. the command palette, architecture rules, macOS 14, string externalization, NFR-REL-02/-04, importer test data (AP-32) |
| D-86 | Should requirements the MVP needs    | Moved into MVP milestones: read-only source view (FR-EDT-10, for NFR-REL-03), hotkey display in Settings (FR-SET-04, D-71), search operators (FR-SRC-04), trash auto-empty (FR-HIS-02), outline (FR-EDT-11) (AP-33) |
| D-87 | Unscheduled requirements             | Should requirements with no phase go to v1.0; Could requirements with no phase are listed as an explicit backlog in §11 (AP-34) |
| D-88 | Notion import detail priority       | FR-IMP-12 and FR-IMP-13 are **Must within FR-IMP-02** (Notion import, C, v1.x): required when Notion import is built, not in the MVP |
| D-89 | System types list                    | The system types are **Page, Task, Quick Note, Daily Note, Tag, Image, PDF** everywhere (D-22, D-42, FR-VLT-02) (AP-35) |
| D-90 | System type definitions              | A new vault gets one `.tegg/types/<type>.yml` per system type, so the vault is self-describing (FR-STO-06, NFR-POR-03). If a system type's file is missing, built-in defaults apply and the file is written only when the user changes that type (FR-SYN-06) (AP-36) |
| D-91 | Opening a folder that isn't a Tegg vault | The app asks whether to **import** the folder and runs the matching importer (an Obsidian vault → the Obsidian importer, FR-IMP-01). Nothing is written before the user confirms (FR-SYN-06) (owner decision on AP-37; scheduling consequence in AP-39) |
| D-92 | Starter vault                        | MS1 builds the empty vault; the **starter vault moves to MS3**, with the starter types' attributes specified in MS3's ACs. The type is named **Reading Note**. Until templates (FR-TPL, v1.0), a new object copies its type's default template file verbatim (attribute defaults and body, no variables) (AP-38) |
| D-93 | Obsidian import in the MVP          | FR-IMP-01 and FR-STO-10 move from v1.0 into **MS7**, next to the Capacities importer (same job framework, backup and preview). The Obsidian importer also handles plain Markdown folders without `.obsidian/` (untyped notes → Page, FR-TYP-10). Until MS7, opening a non-Tegg folder says "Import isn't available yet" and writes nothing (AP-39, D-91) |
| D-94 | Deferred ACs                         | An AC that needs parts built in a later milestone is marked with that milestone right after its label (`- **AC-2** [MS2] …`). The milestone check verifies it there instead of at its requirement's milestone and lists every deferred AC in a `milestone-deferred` check; `verify release` checks all ACs (AP-40, CONTRACT §9.4) |
| D-95 | Static AC tests                      | AC tests of rules that already hold before implementation (static/structural checks) are committed separately with `Red-First-Exempt: static rule already holds in the skeleton; approved-by: owner` (AP-41) |
| D-96 | New note keys                        | Key order `id, type, title, <attributes in schema order>, created, modified`; `title` always written; `type` is the type name in lower case with spaces as `-` (`page`, `quick-note`), as D-54 (AP-42, dialect §3) |
| D-97 | Unclosed frontmatter                 | An opening `---` that never closes is reported as "frontmatter not closed" at line 1 and the whole file is treated as body (NFR-REL-03); the S1/S4 extraction reference follows (AP-43) |
| D-98 | Missing remembered vault             | The app remembers the vault path even when the folder is gone and names it in the chooser (`last_opened` returns the path and whether it exists) (AP-44, FR-VLT-01 AC-7) |
| D-99 | New-empty vault fixture              | The exact content of a new empty vault is the fixture `tests/fixtures/vaults/new-empty/`, drafted from the BRD and **approved by the owner** before MS1 implementation (AP-45, FR-VLT-02 AC-3) |
| D-100 | Mobile platform open               | The original brief asks only for "desktop and future mobile". Mobile stays a future phase with the **platform open** (supersedes D-02's iOS and Android). The core must stay platform-neutral, checked statically (AR-03 AC-3): no platform-specific API outside a marked platform module with a portable fallback. File-name rules keep iOS and Android validity (FR-FMT-08, NFR-POR-02), because vaults are synced to and opened on other devices |
| D-101 | Search typo tolerance | Search words of **≥ 5 letters** match with one edit (insert, delete, substitute, swap of adjacent letters), ranked below exact matches; words of ≤ 4 letters match exactly only. The switcher's in-order-letter match covers typos there (AP-46, FR-SRC-04) |
| D-102 | Trash in search | Objects in `.trash/` are excluded from search, switcher, filters, backlinks and queries; they are reachable only from the trash view (AP-47) |
| D-103 | Untyped files in search | Files without frontmatter are treated as Page, title = file name without `.md`; they match no `created`/`modified` filter (no file-system dates) (AP-48) |
| D-104 | Date filter time zone | Date filters compare in the machine's current time zone; the written offset is kept in the file (AP-49, FR-SRC-02) |
| D-105 | Switcher ranking | Rank by match tier (exact, prefix, word prefix, substring, in-order letters), then by when the object was last opened in Tegg (stored in Application Support, fallback `modified`). An empty query lists recently opened objects (AP-50, FR-NAV-01) |
| D-106 | Switcher create | A "Create '<query>'" row is always offered (first when nothing matches) with the default type (FR-VLT-05). The switcher matches file names as well as titles and aliases (AP-51) |
| D-107 | Search results | One result per object; title matches rank above body matches; snippet ≤ 160 characters around the first body match; pages of 50 results (AP-52, FR-SRC-03) |
| D-108 | Search query syntax | `"…"` and `„…“` mark phrases; `-` excludes only at the start of a term; no OR operator in the MVP (D-253 ranks unquoted multi-word queries in tiers); everything else is literal (AP-53, FR-SRC-04) |
| D-109 | Attribute filter operators | Equality for select, text and checkbox (case- and diacritic-insensitive); ranges for dates and numbers; other operators wait for the query builder (AP-54, FR-SRC-02) |
| D-110 | Progress and cancel | Progress events at least every 500 ms with done/total. Cancellable: import and bulk jobs before their write phase, and indexing (resumed on next open). The write phase of a job is not cancellable (AP-55, FR-UX-07) |
| D-111 | UI parts of MS2 ACs | FR-SYN-03 AC-2/-3/-4/-6, FR-SYN-04 AC-3 and FR-VLT-03 AC-1 are verified at MS3; MS2 gets core-level ACs: `note:conflict` with both versions and no write, change processing of 500 files, placeholder count (AP-56, D-94) |
| D-112 | Precise conflict ACs | Reload keeps the cursor in the same block, else the nearest preceding one; "nearly identical" = ≥ 90% equal lines after normalizing line endings; after re-`id`ing a copied file, links keep resolving by file or `id`, never by title (AP-57, FR-LNK-14) |
| D-113 | Placeholder detection | iCloud `.<name>.icloud` stubs and the `SF_DATALESS` stat flag. Tested with synthetic stubs and a flag injected through the vault file-system abstraction (AR-04); real providers are checked manually at MS8 (AP-58, FR-VLT-03) |
| D-114 | Shell outside the core workspace | The desktop shell (`app/src-tauri`) has its own Cargo manifest and lock file, outside the core Cargo workspace, so core builds and gates never need the built web assets (`app/dist`) that Tauri embeds at compile time. The shell depends on the core crates by path; its tests run in the `app-shell-test` gate step (owner, 2026-10-12) |
| D-115 | MS3 slices and preview builds | MS3 is delivered as slices **3A** frame and page flow, **3B** read view, **3C** editor and autosave, **3D** types and attributes, **3E** settings and polish. Each slice gets its ACs and red tests before it starts (D-61, per slice), ends with `./verify push` and its ACs green plus an explorer session on a generated vault, and ships a preview build with a test charter to the owner. Owner feedback is triaged into `bug_` tests (T1 stops feature work, D-62), AP points or the coverage doc. The MS3 gate is unchanged and runs once, after 3E (AP-59) |
| D-116 | TBD pages | From MS3 the shell contains every page and panel of the designs (`design/edit-note-page/`). A page whose content belongs to a later phase is a **TBD placeholder** naming the page, its purpose, its milestone and requirement IDs; its sidebar entry, shortcut and command-palette entry already lead to it. A later feature inside a built page is a disabled control marked with its milestone. Placeholders have no ACs; one flow test checks that every route is either built or TBD with a milestone, and each milestone replaces its placeholders (AP-60) |
| D-117 | Read view before the editor | Slice 3B adds a read-only rendered note view on `@tegg/doc` before the editor (3C). It stays in the product as the view for files the editor can't open safely (NFR-REL-03); the raw text view becomes the source view (FR-EDT-10) (AP-61) |
| D-118 | Tegg Preview app | Preview builds install as **Tegg Preview.app** (bundle id `app.tegg.preview`) with its own Application Support folder `~/Library/Application Support/Tegg Preview/`, so preview use and test runs never share an index; ad-hoc signed until the Developer ID exists (AP-62, FR-STO-07) |
| D-119 | Dogfood vaults | Slices 3A–3B are tried on the generated vault and a new real vault (they write only notes the owner creates). From 3C, real notes are written in a vault with a backup (Time Machine or a sync service with file versions), because version history (FR-HIS-03) is v1.0. Imported content waits for MS7; Capacities stays the owner's main tool until then. Claude never reads the owner's vault (D-62) (AP-63) |
| D-120 | Navigation frame in MS3 | MS3 builds the app frame, sidebar, right-panel container and command palette (FR-UX-02, -03) with TBD entries (D-116). FR-NAV-02, -04 and -05 stay in MS5, which completes them (every action in the palette, Home/Inbox/Tasks, Outline); no requirement moves (AP-64) |
| D-121 | Sidebar Types list | Order: Page, Task, Quick Note, Daily Note, then user types by name (D-122), then Image and PDF; Tag is not listed (own Tags section, MS4). Counts are shown from slice 3A; untyped files count as Page (D-103). A `type` value without a definition gets its own entry after the rest, with a "no type definition" marker (AP-65). D-516: Task is no longer in the list (the `Tasks` entry covers it). |
| D-122 | Collation | Name-sorted lists use `Intl.Collator` with the macOS region locale, not the UI language, never code-point order; tests run with `cs` and `en` (AP-66, NFR-OTH-02) |
| D-123 | Type page in 3A | Slice 3A shows a minimal type page: the type's objects sorted by title (D-122), rows open the note; filter, sort, views and `New <Type>` are disabled until 3D; `Type settings` leads to its placeholder (AP-67, FR-TYP-09) |
| D-124 | Milestone labels and overdue placeholders | Registry entries carry a milestone and, within MS3, a slice, shown as "Coming in MS3 (3D)". `./verify milestone --milestone MSn` passes `VERIFY_MILESTONE` to every step; the registry test fails on any TBD or disabled entry due at or before it (AP-68, D-116); extended to MS4 slices by D-292 |
| D-125 | Page-map additions | Search is a built route (⌘⇧F, palette; no extra sidebar entry); Conflicts view TBD MS3 (3C); Back/Forward disabled until MS5 (FR-NAV-03); `New type` disabled until 3D; Quick capture TBD MS5; Pinned TBD MS5 (FR-HOM-02); Saved queries TBD v1.0; Settings sections with their milestones, as in `app-shell.md` R1 and R3 (AP-69) (the Pinned TBD route removed by D-1375) |
| D-126 | Added shortcuts | Toggle sidebar ⌃⌘S, focus mode ⇧⌘\ (Esc also leaves it), Settings ⌘,, Inbox ⌘⇧I. ⌘K is always the command palette, also in the editor; links are inserted with `[[`, the toolbar or ⇧⌘K. Shortcut labels use Apple's modifier order ⌃ ⌥ ⇧ ⌘ (AP-70, §7 table) |
| D-127 | Right panel | Tabs Backlinks (MS4), Outline (MS5), Local graph (v1.0); no Attributes tab, attributes stay at the top of the note (FR-EDT-07, 3D); this settles AP-20. The panel exists only on routes that show an object; elsewhere its toggle is absent and ⌘\ does nothing (AP-71, FR-NAV-05) |
| D-128 | Theme storage | The theme choice is stored per machine in Application Support (FR-STO-07), default System; Tegg Preview keeps its own (D-118). Nothing is written to the vault (AP-72, FR-SET-02) |
| D-129 | Theme contrast | The text-contrast part of FR-UX-08 is pulled into FR-UX-05 for the MVP: every text token on every surface it is used on ≥ 4.5:1 in both themes, checked from the tokens and with axe-core's `color-contrast` rule (the rest of axe stays v1.0). `faint` is used only for disabled controls and decorative icons; placeholder hints use `muted` (AP-73) |
| D-130 | Disabled controls and focus | Later-feature controls use `aria-disabled` (focusable) with the description "Coming in <milestone>" on hover and focus; the palette lists them without running them. After any navigation the focus moves to the new page's heading (AP-74, FR-UX-03) |
| D-131 | ⌘N before the type picker | Until FR-VLT-05 (MS5), ⌘N opens a `New object` dialog with a title field and the vault's default type, picker disabled; ⏎ creates through the same path as the switcher's create item (D-106); an empty title can't be created (AP-75). Superseded by D-520 (no title dialog). |
| D-132 | Launch landing | The last-opened record (Application Support) keeps path and `id`. Launch opens the path if it exists, else the object with that `id`, else (or if it is in `.trash/`) the Home placeholder, with no error dialog; launching never writes to the vault (AP-76, FR-HOM-01) |
| D-133 | Plural forms | Count messages use plural categories chosen with `Intl.PluralRules` (`one`/`few`/`many`/`other`); no message concatenates a number with a word; a Czech test catalog proves it (AP-77, NFR-OTH-02) |
| D-134 | FR-UX-07 AC-6 after 3C | FR-UX-07 AC-6 (keep typing during a long operation) needs the editor and is recorded after slice 3C; 3A verifies the non-modal part automatically (FR-UX-07 AC-7) (AP-78) |
| D-135 | Shell contract and test hooks for 3A | `docs/contracts/ms3-shell.md` defines the 3A IPC (vault create, folder pick, last opened, type definitions, type counts, type list, per-machine UI state, `nav_opened`), accessibility labels, the redefined `ui-ready` mark and `route-ready:` marks. With `TEGG_TEST=1`, `TEGG_TEST_PICK_DIR` answers the folder dialog (empty = cancel) (AP-79) |
| D-136 | Minimum window | The main window's minimum size is 800 × 500 pt. When the centre pane would be under 400 px, the right panel closes by itself and reopens when there is room; the user's choice isn't changed (AP-80, FR-UX-02) |
| D-137 | Commands without a vault | With no vault open, the palette offers only `Create new vault…`, `Open vault…`, `Go to Settings` and the theme commands; other shortcuts do nothing and show no error (AP-81, FR-UX-03) |
| D-138 | Recency counts opens only | Only a note shown in the centre pane counts as opened for switcher recency (D-105) and the launch landing (D-132): the UI calls `nav_opened`; `Core::read_note` no longer records recency, since previews and embeds also read notes (AP-82) |
| D-139 | Theme change keeps context | FR-SET-02 AC-2 checks that a theme change keeps the current route and the focused element; run from the palette, the palette closes and the focus returns to the element that had it. "Keeps the open dialog" is dropped, since no dialog can be open when the theme changes (AP-83) |
| D-140 | Links before previews | Until object previews (FR-LNK-08…12, v1.0), a click or ⏎ on a link opens the linked note; `#Heading` and `#^id` subpaths scroll to their target; ⌘-click acts as a click until tabs (v1.0). With previews, the global setting takes over (AP-84) (superseded by slice P, D-921…D-944) |
| D-141 | Aliases don't resolve links | Aliases never resolve a link: `[[alias]]` with no file of that name is unresolved (dialect §6, FR-LNK-14, as Obsidian). Aliases serve the switcher and link autocomplete, which inserts `[[File\|alias]]` (FR-TYP-08 reworded) (AP-85) |
| D-142 | Broken and date links before MS4 | Until FR-LNK-04 and FR-DLY-08 (MS4), an unresolved link has the broken style of D-73, the description `No note named <target>`, and does nothing when activated (no create); a date link without a Daily Note is never shown as broken and is a disabled control `Coming in MS4` (AP-86); the date-link half ends with 4B (FR-DLY-08, D-321): a date link opens the Daily Note for its date, virtual if missing |
| D-143 | Tag click before tag pages | Until tag pages (FR-TAG-06, MS4), activating a `#tag` opens Search with that tag's filter (FR-SRC-02) (AP-87) |
| D-144 | In-note placeholders | Note, image, PDF and other embeds, query blocks and mindmaps the read view can't show yet are in-note placeholders from registry table R5 (`read-view.md`), checked against their milestones like routes (FR-UX-02 AC-13); a note embed also shows a link chip to its target. 3B reads no image or PDF file (AP-88) |
| D-145 | External links and HTML | `http(s)` and `mailto` links open in the default macOS handler through the shell command `open_external`; the app fetches nothing (NFR-SEC-01); other schemes are plain text. Note HTML is never rendered as HTML, only shown as source text. Math and footnotes are shown as source text until a requirement decides their rendering (AP-89) |
| D-146 | Source view | The source view (FR-EDT-10) is toggled with `⌥⌘U`, is read-only with line numbers, keeps the reading position when toggled, and isn't remembered: every open starts formatted, except files with invalid frontmatter (NFR-REL-03). It stays read-only after 3C (AP-90) |
| D-147 | Attribute strip | The read view shows `description` as a subtitle, then tags, aliases, other attributes in file order, source, review, created, modified. Hidden: `id`, `type`, `title`, `icon`, `cover`, `import-id`, `previous`, `file`, empty values. Reserved keys use catalog labels, other keys their raw name (until 3D's schema labels); dates in the region locale's medium format until FR-SET-03 (3E). Collapsible, state per machine (D-127) (AP-91) |
| D-148 | Open-note time before the editor | NFR-PERF-05 AC-2: in 3B the read view meets 75% of the target (112 ms) to the `note-ready` mark; AC-1 (rendered and editable) is measured with the editor at 3C (AP-92) |
| D-149 | Headings 4–6 | The read view renders `####`…`######` as headings (dialect §1, read generously); whether the editor offers them is decided with 3C's ACs (AP-93) |
| D-150 | 3A note page criterion with 3B | FR-UX-02 AC-14 is reworded to the formatted read view (`read-view.md`), keeping the breadcrumb and no-write parts; R1 and R3 are updated in the commit that builds 3B, whose test change carries `AC-Test-Change` approved by the owner (AP-94) |
| D-151 | Callout types with non-ASCII letters | The owner checks in Obsidian whether `> [!rozpočet]` is a callout. If it is, `@tegg/doc` widens the callout rule to Unicode letters with a round-trip test (AR-06) and the fixture gets a Czech type; if not, dialect §4 records "ASCII callout types only" (AP-95) |
| D-152 | Reference-machine perf checks | App-level perf benchmarks (NFR-PERF-05 AC-2 first) run in their own `qa.config` step; they enforce their target only with `TEGG_PERF_REF=1` on the reference machine (D-59) and otherwise report the p95 and pass (AP-96) |
| D-153 | R5 test location | The R5 registry test lives in `app/test/in_note_placeholders.ac.test.ts`, not in `registry.ac.test.ts` (AP-97) |
| D-154 | Properties component | `read/Properties.tsx` renders the subtitle and the Properties strip from `attributeRows`; the note page places it between the title and `ReadView` (AP-98) |
| D-155 | Relative Markdown links | A Markdown link to a `.md` path resolves relative to the linking note's folder first, then by file name (dialect §6). The UI sends targets in NFC (AP-99) |
| D-156 | Chip text with a subpath | A link with a subpath and no alias shows `<title> › <heading>` (block ids: `<title>` only) (AP-100) |
| D-157 | calloutType input | `calloutType(raw)` takes the callout's first line (`> [!type]±` and the title) (AP-101) |
| D-158 | Source lines | A final newline doesn't produce an empty last line element; the line-number gutter is inside each `data-line` element, `aria-hidden` and not selectable (AP-102) |
| D-159 | Fold button name | A callout's fold button is named `Expand <title>` / `Collapse <title>` (type label when untitled) (AP-103) |
| D-160 | Date-times in the strip | Date-times are shown as medium date plus short time in the region locale (`__TEGG_REGION__`), in the machine's zone (D-104) (AP-104) |
| D-161 | Rename while open | FR-SYN-03 AC-11 checks the breadcrumb and the reload from the new path; the tab shows the title, which a rename doesn't change (AP-105) |
| D-162 | Date links first | In `resolve_links`, a `YYYY-MM-DD` target is checked as a date before the path/id/stem rules: kind `daily` (or `date-missing`) (AP-106) |
| D-163 | Generated-vault sample | FR-EDT-02 AC-12 samples 200 notes of a small generated vault created once per test run in a temp dir (or `TEGG_REF_VAULT`) (AP-107) |
| D-164 | Logo without a font | The app logo (Main board: mark and "TEGG" wordmark in Space Grotesk 700, brand blue) ships as two SVG assets, light and dark, with the wordmark's letters converted to paths from Space Grotesk (SIL OFL); no font file ships and nothing is loaded from the network (NFR-SEC-01) (AP-108) |
| D-165 | Back/Forward in MS3 (3B.1) | FR-NAV-03 moves from MS5 to MS3, slice 3B.1: an in-memory route history (with note fragment and scroll position), driven by the title-bar Back/Forward buttons, ⌘[ / ⌘], the mouse back/forward buttons and the trackpad swipe, and in the palette. Not kept across launches. Amends D-125 (AP-109) |
| D-166 | Back/Forward command names | ⌘[ / ⌘] run the palette commands `Go back` / `Go forward`; R4 names them so (AP-110) |
| D-167 | Shortcuts that do nothing | FR-UX-03 AC-9: only ⌘T does nothing and writes nothing; ⌘[ / ⌘] navigate (D-165) (AP-111) |
| D-168 | Outgoing link in Cíl | The read-view fixture's `Cíl.md` ends with `Odkaz ven: [[Odkazy]]`, the link FR-NAV-03 AC-4 follows (AP-112) |
| D-169 | Fragments in history | A route with a fragment is a different route string: `note/Pages/Cíl.md` and `note/Pages/Cíl.md#^blok1` are separate history entries (AP-113) |
| D-170 | Swipe check | The trackpad swipe is checked manually only (FR-NAV-03 AC-3); no synthesized-wheel test (AP-114) |
| D-171 | History start | On every launch the history starts with the landing route as its only entry; the real-app relaunch test reads the title-bar buttons through Appium's `enabled`, confirmed on its first run (AP-115) |
| D-172 | Contract checks in AC tests | The logo tests also check the brand colours of ms3-shell §12.3, the history tests the `data-command` values of §12.2 (AP-116) |
| D-173 | Title editing in 3C | Title editable in 3C, writing only `title:`; file name and links change with renames in MS4; focus lands on the title as a heading (AP-117) |
| D-174 | First edit without `id` | First edit adds `id`, `type: page` (if missing) and `modified` after existing keys; never `title` or `created` (AP-118) |
| D-175 | Version comparison | File versions compare by size and content hash; mtime only triggers re-hashing; identical bytes are not a conflict (changes ms1-core §3.2, red test first) (AP-119) |
| D-176 | Merge dialog | Keep mine / Keep theirs / Keep both / Compare; Esc doesn't close; Keep both writes `<stem> (conflict <YYYY-MM-DD HH-mm>).md` with the same `id`; quit asks, then keeps both (AP-120) |
| D-177 | Conflicts view | Sidebar entry with count while conflicts exist; the link-target file survives; merge edits a draft written on Done; NFC/NFD-only pairs get the same actions (AP-121) |
| D-178 | Undo scope | Per note while shown, cleared on leave and external reload; undo never creates, deletes, moves or renames files; a first-edit `id` stays (AP-122) |
| D-179 | Autosave | Debounce with 1 s max wait; save states table R9; write errors keep edits, show Not saved + Try again, retry every 5 s, ask before quit; ⌘S saves now; no crash journal (AP-123) |
| D-180 | Invalid UTF-8 | `note_read` reports `utf8`; such files open read-only and are never written (AP-124) |
| D-181 | Source blocks | HTML, math, footnotes, comments, unknown fences are read-only in the editor but movable, duplicable, deletable as whole blocks (AP-125) |
| D-182 | Headings 4–6 | Menus offer H1–H3; `####`…`######` creates H4–H6; existing ones stay editable (AP-126) |
| D-183 | Editor shortcuts | Table R8 in `docs/acceptance/editor.md`; Duplicate has no shortcut (⌘D is Today) (AP-127) |
| D-184 | Link autocomplete output | Shortest resolving target (file name, else folder path); `|title` when it differs, `|alias` when matched by alias; typed `]]` makes a link; Create uses the default type and survives undo (AP-128) |
| D-185 | Tag autocomplete before MS4 | Tags from the index, most frequent spelling, default colour; no `Tags/` file until MS4 (AP-129) |
| D-186 | Paste rules | Text as Markdown, ⇧⌘V literal; HTML keeps structure and marks; images become alt text; scripts/styles/frames dropped; only http(s)/mailto links; clipboard image shows the MS6 notice and writes nothing (AP-130) |
| D-187 | Copy link to block | Disabled with "Coming in v1.0" until block ids (FR-LNK-06) (AP-131) |
| D-188 | Multi-block selection | Esc selects the block; ⇧↑/⇧↓ extend; ⇧-click range; mouse drag stays text selection; adjacent paragraphs to list make one list (AP-132) |
| D-189 | Slash menu | Table R6, Objects group first, later items disabled with milestone; callout submenu with 13 Obsidian types; table row/column actions in the block menu (AP-133) |
| D-190 | Typing-latency gate | Gated at 60 Hz; native ProMotion rate reported without a target (AP-134) |
| D-191 | Small editor behaviours | Source toggle saves first; same source text = same block after reload; ⌘Z after auto-conversion restores typed text; empty paragraphs never written; caret and hint text as in editor.md (AP-135); part (e), focus on open, superseded by D-247 and D-255 |
| D-192 | Checkbox toggle | Patches the single marker character, never re-renders the list (AP-136) |
| D-193 | 3C contract | ms3-shell §13 defines note_write, utf8 flag, index_tags, conflict commands, note:conflict, note-ready, labels, test hooks, fake-core controls and the @tegg/editor API before the red tests (AP-137) |
| D-194 | Exact-path create | `note_create_exact(path, text)` creates a file at an exact path, failing on a taken name; `Keep both` appends ` (2)` itself (AP-138) |
| D-195 | Trash from the Conflicts view | `note_trash(path, base)` with a version check, used only by the Conflicts view until MS6 (AP-139) |
| D-196 | Quit flush limit | The shell waits up to 2 s for the flush, then shows `Quit without saving?` (AP-140) |
| D-197 | `note-saved` mark | Stays once per process; L6 waits on file content for later saves (AP-141) |
| D-198 | Core signatures for 3C | `Core::read_note` → `NoteRead { bytes, version, utf8 }`; `Core::tags() -> Vec<TagInfo>`; `Core::create_exact(path, bytes) -> Result<FileVersion>`; `Core::trash_note(path, base) -> Result<String>` (AP-142) |
| D-199 | Latency probe | `createLatencyProbe({ nextFrame, log }) → { keydown(), painted() }` in @tegg/editor; L5 switch `window.__TEGG_TEST__.latencyProbe`; reports `perf_mark {mark:'key',frame,ms}` (AP-143) |
| D-200 | Already-green L4 checks | Added as regression tests in the build commit (AP-144) |
| D-201 | `stemResolves` | `LinkChoice.stemUnique` is renamed `stemResolves`: the stem resolves to that file from `fromPath` (AP-145) |
| D-202 | Conflict error shape | `note_write` Conflict error is `{kind:'Conflict', message, path, mine, theirs, theirsVersion}` (AP-146) |
| D-203 | Switcher created date | `SwitcherItemDto` gains `created: string \| null` (AP-147) |
| D-204 | Clipboard in L5 | L5 dispatches synthetic clipboard events; ⇧⌘V = keys then a paste event (AP-148) |
| D-205 | Unsaved-content equality | FR-SYN-03 AC-16 compares disk with the editor's `text()` (AP-149) |
| D-206 | Foldable fixture callout | Deset bloků's callout becomes `[!tip]+` (AP-150) |
| D-207 | NFC/NFD pair in the fake | Fake core stays NFC; FR-SYN-04 AC-12 keep results are checked in L6/L4 only (AP-151) |
| D-208 | `index-idle` mark | A repeatable `index-idle` perf mark under `TEGG_PERF_LOG` when the index queue drains (AP-152) |
| D-209 | ⌘↩ title | `Open link or toggle checklist item` (AP-153) |
| D-210 | Command context route | `commands(ctx)` gets `ctx.route: { id, path? }` (AP-154) |
| D-211 | Editor test assumptions | `readonlyBlocks()` returns @tegg/doc block indices; editor top-level nodes map 1:1 to body blocks; title is outside the editor document; L1 `faults` read from `globalThis` (AP-155) |
| D-212 | Typing perf note in L5 | L5 uses a synthetic 5,000-word Czech note; the reference-vault note is used in L6 (AP-156) |
| D-213 | Quit dialogs | Quit and window-close dialogs are tested in L6 only (AP-157) |
| D-214 | Editor package and the DOM | AR-03 AC-2's DOM clause applies to the platform-neutral packages (`packages/doc`); `packages/editor` is a UI package and may use the DOM; it still never imports `@tauri-apps/*` or `app/` (AP-158) |
| D-215 | 3B tests after 3C | The 3B read-view WebKit tests run against the read view through the test-only switch `window.__TEGG_TEST__.readView` (the read view ships as the fallback, D-117); the editor's matching look is FR-EDT-02 AC-16; 3B-only assertions (the disabled Edit control) are retired (AP-159) |
| D-216 | `read_note_info` | D-198 amended: `Core::read_note` keeps its tuple; `Core::read_note_info` returns `NoteRead { bytes, version, utf8 }` (AP-160) |
| D-217 | No object icon or cover | Objects have no icon or cover of their own; the icon comes from the object's type everywhere. FR-TYP-14 removed (W); supersedes D-39. `icon`/`cover` stay reserved keys, preserved but not shown; the note header's Add icon / Add cover controls are removed (owner, 3C preview feedback) |
| D-218 | Tag click | Activating a `#tag` (body or properties) opens the tag's page; until tag pages exist (FR-TAG-06, MS4) that is the Tags route placeholder. Supersedes D-143 (owner, 3C preview feedback) (route `tag/<key>`, D-703) |
| D-219 | Vault switcher in the title bar | The title bar shows the open vault as a pill (colour dot + name) before Back/Forward; it opens the menu `Vaults`: the recently opened vaults (machine-local list in Application Support, most recent first, at most 9, each with name, folder path and `⌃⌘1…9`; the current one checked; a folder that no longer exists is shown dimmed with `Not found` and can't be chosen), then `Open folder as vault…` (folder picker), `Create new vault…` (New vault form) and `Vault settings…` (Settings: Vault). The dot's colour is derived from the folder path (fixed palette, nothing stored). One vault stays open at a time (FR-VLT-04). Owner, 2026-10-14, design boards option C |
| D-220 | Logo at the bottom left | The logo (mark + TEGG wordmark, D-164) moves to the bottom left of the sidebar, below the scrolling list, as a button `Tegg: About and Settings` opening a menu: `About Tegg` (the standard macOS About panel) and `Settings…` (Settings: Appearance, `⌘,` unchanged). It no longer links to Home (Home stays in the sidebar list and the palette). Supersedes the logo link of FR-UX-02 AC-2/AC-5 (D-164 assets unchanged). Owner, 2026-10-14 |
| D-221 | Renaming a type | The key is fixed when the type is created; Name and Plural are display text. Renaming changes no file but the type file (`type: idea` stays). (AP-161) |
| D-222 | Type names and folders | Reject names and plurals with the D-52/D-68 characters or control characters; key unique (case-insensitive, NFC) incl. system types; key ≤ 200 UTF-8 bytes; folder relative, inside the vault, no `..`, no segment starting with `.`, not `_templates`/`_attachments`/`.trash`/`.tegg` or under them, unique among types; nested folders allowed. A key equal to an undefined `type` value adopts those objects. (AP-162) |
| D-223 | Property keys | Derived from the Name (NFC, lower case, whitespace as `-`, forbidden characters removed, diacritics kept) until edited by hand; letters of any script, digits, `-`, `_`, starting with a letter; not reserved (FR-TYP-15), unique in the type, no date-range collisions; fixed after creation (~~renames MS4~~ never renamed, D-806). (AP-163) |
| D-224 | Type change in MS3 (FR-TYP-07 split) | 3D writes only `type:` (and `modified`); the file stays in its folder until MS4 moves it (MS4 also offers to move objects that are outside their type's folder). | D-225 | Deleting a type in MS3 | 3D deletes only types with no objects (trashed objects don't count); the type file and its `_templates/<Name>/` go to `.trash/` with relative paths kept. With objects, the two choices are disabled `Coming in MS4` (new FR-TYP-05 AC-4 [MS4]). (AP-165) |
| D-226 | Changing a type's folder (settles AP-17 for MS3) | 3D: the new folder applies to new objects; `Move existing objects` is disabled `Coming in MS4`; split data-safety AC-2 so its "offers to move" part is `[MS4]`; change the board's help text. (AP-166) |
| D-227 | Changing a property's type and its options (settles AP-18 for MS3) | 3D: retyping allowed after a confirmation with the count of values that won't fit; files untouched, misfits shown as invalid.
